## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:15Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Decision**: Q1: Component decomposition inside oxidean-vcs — one engine or bounded parts?

---

## Human Turn
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:15Z
**Event**: HUMAN_TURN

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:15Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Details**: Q1 B — decompose by data ownership + change rate: ObjectStore (CAS bytes+refcounts), ChangesetStore, RefRegistry, ReadIndex (derived data: log/diff/tree/blame/history), MergeEngine, GitInterop, WorkingCopy (client sparse fetch/materialize/cache), plus thin wire adapters (CLI, MCP descriptors, ActivityRecorder). Storage is stable, merge/interop evolve, client logic moves with the CLI — eight bounded components keeps seams testable and entity ownership unambiguous. [standing instruction: conductor answers from evidence]

---

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:20Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Decision**: Q2: Where do vcs.* wire types and proc_classes entries live?

---

## Human Turn
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:20Z
**Event**: HUMAN_TURN

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:20Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Details**: Q2 B — wire DTOs + proc_classes entries live in oxidean-core (highest-fan-out leaf; putting them in the crate would invert the core<-leaf dependency rule). [standing instruction]

---

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:20Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Decision**: Q3: Server topology — in-app mount or aux listener?

---

## Human Turn
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:21Z
**Event**: HUMAN_TURN

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:21Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Details**: Q3 B — mount inside router_with_state: RPC procedures join PROCEDURES/dispatch, object routes nest under /api/vcs/{o}/{r}/objects. The ratified /api/ prefix decision already gives zero edge-parity work; an aux listener would duplicate auth/CORS/wire-version plumbing. [standing instruction]

---

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:21Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Decision**: Q4: Persistence split — DB tables vs filesystem CAS?

---

## Human Turn
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:21Z
**Event**: HUMAN_TURN

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:21Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Details**: Q4 B — metadata/lineage/refs/derived indexes in oxidean-db tables x3 dialects; blob bytes in content-addressed OXIDEAN_VCS_OBJECTS_DIR (sha-sharded LFS/packages pattern) with refcount + grace-windowed GC job. Keeps DB rows small, want-based GETs stream from disk. [standing instruction]

---

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:21Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Decision**: Q5: Entity ownership map

---

## Human Turn
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:21Z
**Event**: HUMAN_TURN

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:21Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Details**: Q5 — Changeset, FileChange, ObjectEntry, Ref, ImportLink, DerivedIndex rows owned by their VCS components; CheckoutCache owned by WorkingCopy (never crosses wire); repo_activity rows NOT owned — ActivityRecorder calls Database::insert_repo_activity facade per the dependency-correct FR16 path. [standing instruction]

**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED

**Decision**: Q6: How does git_export reach the protection-verdict path without oxidean-vcs depending on oxidean-api?

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:25Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Details**: Q6 A — oxidean-vcs defines a ProtectionChecker trait (port); oxidean-api's dispatch arm supplies the real implementation (existing protection hook). Trait-in-crate preserves host->crate direction, matches the trait+adapter team idiom, keeps export logic shareable by CLI/tests. [standing instruction]

---

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:25Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Decision**: Q7: Derived-data computation — on-write, on-read, or job-based?

---

## Human Turn
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:25Z
**Event**: HUMAN_TURN

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:25Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Details**: Q7 B — incremental on-write: changeset create transactionally updates blame/path_history index rows from parent state; reads hit index tables (O(index)); GC/compaction is a background job. No async consistency window; if hot-path latency suffers later, derivation can move to a job without changing the wire contract. [standing instruction]

---

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:26Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Decision**: Q8: Does client working-copy (sparse checkout/materialize) logic live in the crate?

---

## Human Turn
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:26Z
**Event**: HUMAN_TURN

---

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:47:26Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Details**: Q8 Yes — WorkingCopy owns manifest filtering, local object cache, and materialization (mode/symlink fidelity); oxidean-cli holds thin argv glue and gains a dependency on oxidean-vcs (same pattern as its existing oxidean-core dep). [standing instruction]

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:55:57Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED

**Decision**: Learnings selection for domain-design

**Event**: HUMAN_TURN

## Question Answered
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-18T04:55:57Z
**Event**: QUESTION_ANSWERED
**Stage**: domain-design
**Details**: Keep c1 (two-case flag-gate semantics: absent row denies all, enabled=false denies writes only — the model for every per-repo feature flag), c2 (global CAS row + per-repo link rows, referenced-ness computed at GC — the durable content-store pattern), c3 (client/server cargo feature split when one crate serves lean CLI + heavy server). Skip c4/c6/c7 (run-specific decisions, artifact-captured) and c5 (duplicates the earlier /api/-prefix learning). [standing instruction]
